# Encoder 1301, world 3: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1301_w03_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `4b5cf6a19d4bcf79b6c5a020c6361451604d79e22d46d1b419180cf26ad9af97`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 provides fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Use origins/times as supplied: copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional, not executed events. Current (represented_time 12) summary: n0 is uniform for both roles (all 16 cells equal). n1: blue peaks on the top-right 2×2 (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)); red peaks on the top-left 2×2 (cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). n2: blue peaks on the top-left 2×2; red peaks on the top-right 2×2. n3: blue peak at (1,2); red peak at (0,1). Conditional command candidates k0 and k1 reproduce the same peak patterns as current for all nodes. Candidate k2 differs only at n3: its blue peak is at (0,3) instead of (1,2); red peaks remain at (0,1).</pre>

## 02. s1301_w03_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2200f9dfb4dc2f82d091a45db092f5cf28df1fec5f353f02087296d161471cf9`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds fallible, grid-based probability distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Origins: distributions labeled copied_prior_record are prior records (not new estimates); items labeled as command estimates or candidate are conditional (not executed events). Current estimates (represented_time 12) in p0: at n0 both roles are uniform across the 4×4 grid. At n1 blue mass is concentrated on cells in rows 0–1 and columns 2–3 while red mass is concentrated on rows 0–1 and columns 0–1. At n2 the roles are swapped (blue concentrated on rows 0–1 columns 0–1; red on rows 0–1 columns 2–3). At n3 blue peaks at cell (1,2) and red peaks at (0,1) with very high peak probabilities. Candidate conditional records for commands k0, k1 and k2 reproduce the same peak cell sets as the current distributions; their numeric probabilities differ slightly in some cases (notably at n3 across commands).</pre>

## 03. s1301_w03_t12_A_A_only_current_restored

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `fd165a89ae31211088957857e066caaf86538d6d0ffa172ba04c6f3523236419`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current distributions: nodes n0–n2 for both red and blue are copies of a prior record and should not be taken as new estimates; n0 is uniform for both roles. At n1 blue mass concentrates on cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red concentrates on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). At n2 blue and red peak sets are swapped relative to n1. At n3 the current blue peak is cell (1,2) and red peak is cell (0,1). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional estimates (not executed events). k0 and k1 reproduce the same peak sets as current at all nodes. k2 differs at n3: its blue peak shifts to cell (0,3) (different expected row/column and lower peak probability than the current n3 blue peak); red at n3 remains peaked at (0,1). These statements follow the supplied origins and represented times.</pre>

Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are not events that have already occurred.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 provides fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented_time 12) for n0 are uniform for both roles (all 16 cells equal); these are copied prior records (record_time 11) and are not new estimates. Current n1 concentrates blue on cells (row,col) (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). Current n2 swaps those concentrations (blue on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); red on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)). Current n3 (history_estimate, represented_time 12, feedback_through 11) peaks blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Candidate records conditioned on commands k0 and k1 reproduce the current peaks at all nodes; they are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred. Candidate record for k2 matches current peaks at n0–n2 but differs at n3: blue peaks at (0,3) under k2 while red still peaks at (0,1). No claim about physical presence or absolute truth is made; numbers report distributional peaks only.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 (current): n0 is uniform for both roles. n1: blue mass on the upper-right 2x2 (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)); red mass on the upper-left 2x2 (cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). n2: blue mass on the upper-left 2x2; red mass on the upper-right 2x2. n3: blue peaks at (1,2); red peaks at (0,1). Panel p0 (candidate commands k0,k1,k2) preserves these peak locations for nodes n0–n2; node n3 shows command-dependent concentration strength (some commands concentrate strongly on (1,2) for blue and (0,1) for red, others distribute more). Panel p1 (current): n0 uniform; n1 and n2 show the same alternating 2x2 peak patterns as p0; n3 is uniform. Panel p1 (candidates) likewise preserves those patterns for n1–n2 while n3’s concentration varies by command. Origins indicate copied prior records and conditional estimates; conditional records are not executed events.</pre>

## 09. s1301_w03_t12_A_joined_current_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `6805beed876ef93f8775d1e3314cc28c424dd30985a92c2131e27de2641729ad`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on 4×4 local grids at nodes n0–n3, in panels p0 and p1. Copied prior records (record_time 11 → represented_time 12) are retained but are not new estimates; conditional command records (represented_time 13) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current estimates: node n0 is uniform for both roles in both panels; node n1 shows blue mass on the top-right two columns (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) and red mass on the top-left two columns (cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)); node n2 shows the opposite pattern (blue on top-left four, red on top-right four). In panel p0 the current estimate at n3 concentrates on cell (1,2) for blue and (0,1) for red with very high peak probabilities. In panel p1 the n3 current distribution is uniform; candidate command records for n3 vary across commands (some commands produce a strong single-cell peak, others multi-cell peaks or uniform). Other nodes are stable across command-conditioned candidates.</pre>

## 10. s1301_w03_t12_A_joined_current_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `b47d6ef275c5d50f2dcfbdb327c5f79eb9a39ff3dbb02d226e93b611aceac844`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue over 4x4 local grids at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current estimates: in both panels node n0 is uniform for both roles. Nodes n1 and n2 in both panels show complementary partial-column concentration: blue peaks in the right two columns of the top two rows and red peaks in the left two columns of the top two rows (each role peaked on four cells). In panel p0 current node n3, blue peaks at cell [1,2] and red peaks at [0,1]. In panel p1 current node n3 is uniform. Conditional candidate records vary: some candidates leave distributions like the current ones, while others shift panel p1 node n3 — e.g. candidate k0 concentrates blue on the right half of the top two rows and red on the left half; candidate k1 or k2 concentrate blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1], with k2 showing stronger concentration. Copied or inherited records are not new measurements; conditional command records are hypothetical estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 11. s1301_w03_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible, grid-based location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current (inherited/completed) estimates: n0 is uniform in both roles; n1 has blue concentrated on columns 2–3 of top rows and red concentrated on columns 0–1 of top rows; n2 shows the complementary pattern (blue on left/top, red on right/top); p0 current n3 is sharply concentrated (blue at cell [1,2], red at [0,1]); p1 current n3 is uniform. Conditional command records are hypothetical, not executed events; copied prior records are inherited, not new estimates. Across candidate commands, the n1/n2 patterns are consistent; n3 varies by panel and command: some command-conditioned estimates place blue on the right and red on the left in p1 (k0), some leave p1 n3 uniform (k1), and others concentrate p1 or p0 n3 at [1,2] (blue) and [0,1] (red). No physical, presence or subjective inferences are made; probabilities and uncertainty refer only to the supplied distributions.</pre>

## 12. s1301_w03_t12_A_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `8016bb6de93f93a873fd08a0f8def1156d91f6b885f293a651926af13d1d45bb`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on four addressed nodes in two panels. Use origins/times as supplied: copied-prior/completed records are inherited, not new estimates, and conditional/command records are conditional (not executed events). Panel p0 current: n0 is uniform for both roles; n1 concentrates blue on top-right two columns of the top two rows and red on top-left two columns of the top two rows; n2 is the opposite; n3 concentrates blue at cell (1,2) and red at (0,1). Panel p0 candidate commands k0–k2 show the same peak cells as current for these nodes. Panel p1 current: n0 is uniform; n1 and n2 match p0&#x27;s asymmetric peaks; n3 is uniform. Panel p1 candidate commands differ at n3: k0 peaks place blue on the top-right two columns of the top two rows and red on the top-left two columns; k1 leaves n3 uniform for both roles; k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). No propositions infer physical presence or realized events; peaks and differences describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

## 13. s1301_w03_t12_A_joined_W_changed

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `e357ff5830e9eb07dbd2bf7c893323eacd0e75f04c8362f8d1b50f27d028215e`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions over 4x4 local grids for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current statements: in both panels p0 and p1 node n0 distributions for both roles are uniform across all 16 cells. Nodes n1 and n2 show complementary mass: in both panels blue peaks are on cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red peaks are on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) for n1; n2 swaps those peak sets. Node n3 current in p0 is concentrated on blue (1,2) and red (0,1); in p1 current n3 places blue mass on the four-cell set (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). Candidate records under commands k0, k1, k2 show alternative conditional estimates; for example p1 node n3 ranges across candidates from uniform (k1) to the four-cell blue peak (k0/current) to a strong single-cell blue peak at (1,2) under k2. Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are not events that have occurred.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0: current (represented_time 12; copied prior entries are not new estimates) and three conditional command estimates k0–k2 (conditional estimates for represented_time 13; not executed events) provide 4×4 distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current summary: n0 is uniform for both roles; n1 concentrates blue on cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3] and red on [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; n2 has blue on the top-left 2×2 and red on the top-right 2×2; n3 has a blue peak at [1,2] and a red peak at [0,1]. Candidate conditionals k0, k1 and k2 reproduce the same peak cells at n0–n2 and keep single-cell peaks at n3, but they differ in peak probabilities and small redistributed mass at n3 (k1 shows the largest blue peak probability at [1,2], k0 the smallest).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 distributions and supplied origins/times: the current records (nodes n0–n2 are copied prior records, represented_time 12; n3 is a history estimate, represented_time 12) show: n0 — both roles uniform over the 4×4 grid; n1 — blue peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) and red peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); n2 — blue and red peaks swapped relative to n1 (blue at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); red at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)); n3 — blue peak at (1,2), red peak at (0,1). Candidate conditional estimates (k0, k1, k2) are not executed events. Nodes n0, n1 and n2 have the same peaks across all candidates; node n3 differs by command: under k0 both roles peak at (3,3); under k1 n3 matches the current peaks; under k2 blue peaks at (1,3) while red peaks at (3,3).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions for n0–n2 are inherited from a copied prior record (record_time 11, represented_time 12) and shown here as current; n3’s current distribution is a history estimate feedback_through 11 (represented_time 12). Candidate distributions k0, k1, k2 are conditional command estimates (represented_time 13) and are not executed events. Current summary: n0 uniform for both roles; n1 has blue mass concentrated in columns 2–3 rows 0–1 while red concentrates in columns 0–1 rows 0–1; n2 shows the complementary left/right pattern; n3’s current peaks differ from candidates. Across candidates n3 varies: under k0 both roles peak at [3,3]; under k1 n3 matches the current peaks; under k2 blue peaks at [1,3] while red peaks at [3,3]. No physical, presence or confidence claims are made; these are distributional summaries only.</pre>

## 22. s1301_w03_t12_ordinary_joined_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `eef1e112fddf5bc86e1551d85017b7f0f2803fb5e88f97b17da3847edd44c991`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Panel p0 current (represented time 12) shows n0 uniform for both roles; n1: blue concentrated on the top-right 2×2, red on the top-left 2×2; n2: blue on top-left 2×2, red on top-right 2×2; n3: blue peaks at (1,2), red peaks at (0,1). Panel p0 candidate command records mostly match current for n0–n2 but differ at n3: k0 peaks for both roles at (3,3); k1 matches the current pattern at n3; k2 places blue at (1,3) and red at (3,3). Panel p1 current mirrors the top-node patterns for n1–n2, with n0 and n3 uniform. Panel p1 candidate records vary at n3: k0 peaks for both roles at (3,3); k1 remains uniform; k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Copied prior records and conditional command records are labeled in the origins and are not events claimed to have occurred.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current estimates (represented_time 12): nodes n0–n2 show the same structured distributions: n0 uniform for both roles; n1’s blue mass concentrates on the top-right 2×2 and red on the top-left 2×2; n2’s blue concentrates on the top-left 2×2 and red on the top-right 2×2. p0 current n3 concentrates blue at cell [1,2] and red at [0,1]. Panel p0 candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) inherit prior records for n0–n2 (no change from current) while n3’s candidate estimates vary in peak probability across commands though peaks remain at [1,2] (blue) and [0,1] (red). Panel p1 current estimates: n0 uniform; n1 blue top-right 2×2 and red top-left 2×2; n2 blue top-left 2×2 and red top-right 2×2; n3 uniform. Panel p1 candidate records differ by command for n3: k0 shifts peaks to blue top-right 2×2 and red top-left 2×2; k1 leaves n3 uniform; k2 concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1]. Copied prior records are noted as inherited, and conditional command records are presented as hypothetical command-conditioned estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 25. s1301_w03_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `8d27aa49cff43c364c3e2a1fd98ec36be3f8f690d1a4d8cd8268c1523d688084`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) contain 4×4 discrete-location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates: in both panels n0 is uniform over all 16 cells. At n1 blue concentrates on the upper-right 2×2 (columns 2–3, rows 0–1) while red concentrates on the upper-left 2×2 (columns 0–1, rows 0–1). At n2 the blue and red roles are swapped relative to n1. Panel p0 current n3 places blue peak at cell [1,2] and red peak at [0,1]. Panel p1 current n3 is uniform. Conditional candidate records (commands k0,k1,k2) preserve the same peak patterns at n1 and n2; they differ mainly at n3 across commands and panels. For example, several command-conditioned distributions concentrate blue at [1,2] and red at [0,1], with peak probabilities varying (e.g. ~0.998–0.999 across k0–k2 in p0 and p1 where concentrated). Copied or inherited origins and conditional-record semantics are noted in the packet; these are data records, not executed events.</pre>

## 26. s1301_w03_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `ada595fa4c525e22fa40306c0180ba10dc38b986cc1c8ed6ba82e9f17ea47de8`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet shows fallible location distributions (4x4 grid) for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates (copied prior records, represented_time 12) are not new measurements. Conditional command records (candidate k0/k1/k2, represented_time 13) are hypothetical, not executed events. Summary: for both panels the current distribution at n0 is uniform for both roles. At n1 current blue concentrates on the right-half top two rows (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) while red concentrates on the left-top quadrant (cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). At n2 current roles are complementary to n1. Node n3 shows variation: current estimates place blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1) in p0 and p1, while conditional candidates produce different peaks at n3 (examples: both roles at (3,3) under some commands, or strong single-cell concentration at (1,2) or (1,3) in others). Do not infer physical presence or event occurrence from these probability peaks; they describe only the distributions supplied.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) present fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 (rows increase down, columns right). Copied-prior records (represented_time 12) are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional, not executed events. Current (non-conditional) summaries: p0 current — n0 uniform for both roles; n1 blue mass on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); n2 blue on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) while red on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); n3 blue concentrated at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Conditional candidate records differ by command: e.g. p0 k0 places both roles at (3,3) in n3; other branches concentrate roles on different single or small sets of cells (for example p1 k2 concentrates blue at (1,2) and red at (0,1)).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1). Each panel gives separate 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records: p0 shows n0 uniform; n1 blue concentrated on cells (row 0–1, col 2–3) and red on (row 0–1, col 0–1); n2 is the mirror (blue on cols 0–1, red on cols 2–3); p0 n3 has a blue peak at [1,2] and a red peak at [0,1]. Panel p1 current has n0 uniform, n1/n2 with the same column-localized peaks as p0, and n3 uniform. Candidate (conditional) records differ in specific nodes: for p0 candidate k0 both roles at n3 peak at [3,3] while k1/k2 match the current p0 n3 peaks; for p1, candidate k0 moves n3 peaks to the top two columns (rows 0–1, cols 2–3 for blue and cols 0–1 for red), k1 leaves n3 uniform, and k2 places blue at [1,3] and red at [3,3]. Copied prior records are not new estimates, and the conditional command records are conditional estimates, not events that have occurred.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions over 4×4 local grids for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented_time 12) show: n0 uniform for both roles (all 16 cells maximal); n1 blue peaks on cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) while red peaks on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); n2 blue peaks on (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) while red peaks on (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); n3 uniform for both (history_estimate represented_time 12). Origins indicate the copied prior records (record_time 11) are not new estimates. Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2; represented_time 13) are hypothetical branches, not events that have occurred. Across candidates the node-level distributions mostly match current n1–n2 patterns; n3 differs by command: k1 leaves n3 uniform, k0 concentrates n3 on the upper two rows of columns 2–3 for blue and on columns 0–1 for red, and k2 makes n3 highly concentrated at blue cell (1,2) and red cell (0,1).</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0, current estimates (represented_time=12 unless noted):
- n0: both red and blue are uniform across the 4×4 grid (all 16 cells equal). This current distribution was copied from a prior record and is not a new estimate.
- n1: blue concentrates on the upper-right 2×2 block (columns 2–3, rows 0–1); red concentrates on the upper-left 2×2 block (columns 0–1, rows 0–1).
- n2: blue concentrates on the upper-left 2×2 block; red concentrates on the upper-right 2×2 block.
- n3: current (history estimate represented_time=12) shows a strong blue peak at cell (1,2) and a strong red peak at (0,1).
Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2; conditional, not executed events):
- k0 matches current at n0–n2 but leaves n3 uniform.
- k1 matches the current strong peaks at n3 (blue at (1,2), red at (0,1)).
- k2 matches n0–n2 and for n3 places blue on the upper-right 2×2 and red on the upper-left 2×2.
These descriptions report only the supplied distributional peaks and origins; they do not assert physical events or external facts.</pre>

## 31. s1301_w03_t12_analytic_A_only_current_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `0c1e11203a764f8941d09b547fdc63cb2b53c0ec3cdc0e94578fe00441785274`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (represented_time 12) are inherited from a prior record (record_time 11) for n0–n2 and a history_estimate for n3; those copied values are not new estimates. In the current view: n0 has uniform distributions for both roles; n1 places blue mass on columns 2–3 (rows 0–1) and red mass on columns 0–1 (rows 0–1); n2 is the mirror of n1 (blue on columns 0–1, red on 2–3); n3 is uniform. Candidate conditional command records (represented_time 13) are conditional estimates, not executed commands. Across candidates k0–k2 the first three nodes remain as in current. The candidates differ at n3: k0 concentrates blue on columns 2–3 (rows 0–1) and red on columns 0–1 (rows 0–1); k1 leaves both roles uniform at n3; k2 concentrates blue at cell [1,2] and red at cell [0,1].</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented time 12) are copied from a prior record and are not new observations; conditional candidate estimates (commands k0,k1,k2) are conditional branches, not events already executed. Current: n0 is uniform for both roles; n1 concentrates blue on the right half (columns 2–3) and red on the left half (columns 0–1); n2 has blue left-half and red right-half concentrations; n3 concentrates blue at cell (1,2) and red at (0,1). Across candidates: k0 matches the current pattern except n3 is uniform; k1 matches current for n0–n2 and places n3 peaks at blue (1,2) and red (0,1); k2 matches current for n0–n2 and places n3 with blue on the right-half and red on the left-half. No proposition here asserts physical presence, experience, or calibrated confidence—values are distributional summaries only.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 presents fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records (represented_time 12) for n0 and n3 are uniform for both roles; the current n1 blue mass favors columns 2–3 (rows 0–1) while current n1 red favors columns 0–1 (rows 0–1); current n2 shows the opposite split (blue left, red right). Origins: many current entries are copied_prior_record (record_time 11 represented_time 12) and are not new estimates; the n3 current entry is a history_estimate (represented_time 12). Candidate (conditional) command records k0,k1,k2 are conditional estimates (represented_time 13) and are not events that have occurred. Across candidates: node n0 stays uniform; n1 and n2 consistently show the same left/right splits as current; n3 varies by command — k1 leaves n3 uniform, k0 places blue on n3&#x27;s right half and red on its left half, and k2 concentrates blue at cell [1,2] and red at cell [0,1].</pre>

## 36. s1301_w03_t12_analytic_joined_baseline

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `65589983c7c69ec1f282919dfaf9cafb6cbb9e2ed5d037f435f565ec6bd25674`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet has two panels (p0, p1). Each panel gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented times noted in origins) show: n0 distributions are uniform for both roles; n1 concentrates blue on the upper-right 2×2 cells and red on the upper-left 2×2; n2 swaps that pattern (blue upper-left, red upper-right). Panel p0 current n3 is uniform; panel p1 current n3 concentrates blue at cell (1,2) and red at (0,1). Conditional candidate records (commands k0, k1, k2) are hypothetical alternatives, not executed events; copied prior records are inherited, not new estimates. Across commands the node-wise patterns are stable for n1 and n2; n3 differs by command: some conditional views leave n3 uniform, others make n3 concentrated (blue at (1,2), red at (0,1)). No physical facts or occurrences are inferred.</pre>

## 37. s1301_w03_t12_analytic_joined_current_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `046ecd53da59af829fbf386ac1bc55b91c199f85ec27ed0cd9254fff8b409429`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) provide fallible location distributions over 4x4 local grids for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates: in both panels n0 is uniform for both roles; n1’s blue mass concentrates on top-right column pair (cells in columns 2–3, rows 0–1) while red concentrates on top-left column pair (columns 0–1, rows 0–1); n2 shows the opposite concentrations (blue on top-left, red on top-right). Panels differ at n3: in p0 current the blue peak is at cell [1,2] and the red peak at [0,1]; in p1 current both roles are uniform at n3. Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2) mostly reproduce the current patterns for n0–n2 and change n3 in some branches: k1 in both panels keeps the strong peaks at n3 (blue [1,2], red [0,1]); k2 makes n3 peaks shift to the top-right 2x2 (cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) in the candidate for panel p0 and p1. Copied records and conditional-command entries are labeled as inherited or conditional per their origins and are not events or new unconditional facts.</pre>

<pre>This packet reports fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current (represented times given in the packet): in panel p0, node n0 is uniform for both roles; n1 concentrates blue in columns 2–3 rows 0–1 and red in columns 0–1 rows 0–1; n2 swaps that pattern (blue in columns 0–1, red in 2–3); n3 is uniform. In panel p1 current, n0 is uniform, n1 and n2 match p0’s concentrated quadrants, and n3 concentrates strongly: blue peak at cell [1,2], red peak at [0,1]. Across candidate command records most distributions match current; notable differences appear under candidate k2 for p0 n3 (quadrant concentrations) and for p1 n3 (the concentrated blue [1,2] and red [0,1] estimates). No physical presence, subjective state or calibrated confidence is asserted—these are distributional summaries only.</pre>

## 40. s1301_w03_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `27f0d6508df0bfd015a6603476b09b0a03f3d79ff7191760d68806d3e37b25d9`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible location distributions over 4×4 local grids for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Current records (specified origins/times are preserved in the packet) are not new estimates; conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are offered as alternative, not executed, branches. Summary of current estimates: n0 is uniform for both roles; n1 concentrates for blue on columns 2–3, rows 0–1 and for red on columns 0–1, rows 0–1; n2 shows the converse (blue on left 2×2, red on right 2×2); n3 in current views is uniform in p0 and p1. Across candidate commands most nodes retain the same peak sets as current; notable conditional differences: some command branches (k0,k2) place peaks at n3 with blue on right 2×2 and red on left 2×2, while other branches (k1 for both panels) leave n3 uniform; panel p1, command k2 offers a strong concentration at a single cell for blue (cell [1,2]) and for red (cell [0,1]). No physical facts or events are asserted; these are distributional descriptions only.</pre>

## 41. s1301_w03_t12_analytic_joined_W_changed

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `d1aedfbdaa29b28cb7b2ba76d37dc9774954a1a844171755901826e6244e5f47`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Use the supplied origins and times: copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are hypothetical, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: in both panels n0 distributions are uniform for both roles; n1 and n2 show complementary 2×2 top-corner peaks (p0/p1: blue peaks on the top-right 2×2 and red peaks on the top-left 2×2 at n1; the opposite at n2); n3 is uniform in panel p0 and, in p1 current, the blue peak is at (1,2) and red at (0,1). Candidate conditional records (view=candidate, commands k0,k1,k2) mostly mirror the current patterns per node, but differ at specific command branches: notably panel p1 under command k2 contains very concentrated peaks (blue at [1,2], red at [0,1]) while other commands or panels leave n3 uninformative or with 2×2 peak patterns. The propositions enumerate the peak cells for every supplied role-distribution.</pre>

## 42. s1301_w03_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `349af6f88b02da39b293516f13fccfff0c4497591865ada042570a7b60c28252`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current distributions (some copied from record_time 11 represented_time 12, and a history estimate for n3) are: n0 and n3 uniform over the 4×4 grid; n1 has blue concentrated on the four cells at rows 0–1, columns 2–3 and red on rows 0–1, columns 0–1; n2 is the opposite. Candidate command records for p0 (k0,k1,k2) are conditional (not executed): they differ at n3—k1 leaves n3 uniform while k0 and k2 concentrate probability on the same four-cell peaks. Panel p1 current mirrors p0 for n0–n2 with n3 uniform. Panel p1 candidate records vary at n3: k1 uniform, k0 concentrated on four cells, and k2 concentrates almost all mass to single cells (blue at [1,2], red at [0,1]). Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical, not events that have occurred.</pre>
